# CLAUDE.md — PPP Maturity Check System


This file provides persistent context for Claude Code sessions.
Update it whenever significant decisions are made or status changes.

---

## Project Overview

**Name:** PPP Maturity Check System
**Developer:** Solo (Salim / sjacuru@gmail.com)
**Working directory:** `c:\Users\sanseri\Documents\Projetos\Maturity_Check`
**GitHub:** https://github.com/Sjacuru/Maturity_Check
**Language:** Bilingual PT + EN

A system that evaluates Brazilian public procurement (PPP) documents against the
IPMP framework (Indicador de Percepção de Maturidade de Projetos), producing a
maturity score for each of 46 actions across 5 IPMP dimensions. The evaluation is
human-validated — the system surfaces evidence, the auditor confirms scores.

---

## Strategic Decisions (as of 2026-05-20; #2 and #4 updated 2026-10-15, #8 on 2026-10-16)

1. **Primary reference: IPMP** — operationalises each action into concrete expected
   products, scoring rubrics (0/1/3), and scored examples
   (Atendido / Parcialmente / Não Atendido).
2. **Secondary reference: Rio Manual** — provides document names and procedures
   used as retrieval hints when searching case documents. Populated for Ações 1
   and 2 only (`data/rio_manual/`): for the other 44, `retrieve_document_focused`
   returns None, so cascade steps A/B (the focal route) never fire and retrieval
   goes straight to step C (recorded 2026-10-15).
3. **TCDF IN: dropped.**
4. **Scoring: 0 / 1 / 3 per action** (46 actions, max = 138 total). The case's IPMP
   nota is the points over three times the Ações counted, computed on read (ADR-0075).
5. **Retrieval: BM25 (SQLite FTS5) + dense vector, deterministic.**
   Cascade per Ação: (A) exact document name match → (B) variant name match →
   (C) BM25 and vector fused via per-product Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF,
   k=60) — vector is **co-equal and always-on**, not a fallback, since
   ADR-0049 (superseded ADR-0033's "vector only on zero lexical results").
   `rio_hints` stays a BM25-only lane. Regex additive search (former Step D)
   was removed entirely (ADR-0052).
6. **LLM: temperature=0, fixed prompt per action.** IPMP criteria + scored
   examples embedded in prompt. Human auditor validates every score.
   **Sole provider: the owner's own GPU server** (`189.60.217.20`, reached only through an SSH tunnel on port 443 to server port 8083 via `LLAMACPP_BASE_URL`: the server port 8080 serves another, intermittent service and port 8080 is blocked on the client network, per the user on 2026-10-10; RTX 5070
   Ti, `gpt-oss-20b-F16.gguf` served by `llama-server.exe` = llama.cpp), for both
   the scorer and the pre-scoring relevance gate (ADR-0050, ADR-0059). GPU down
   → the run fails loudly; nothing falls back. The Groq/Ollama circuit-breaker
   chain (ADR-0054) stays in the tree, unwired at exactly one place —
   `main.py`'s `_bootstrap()` — "para mostrar que foi tentado".
   **Naming rule (2026-10-02):** "llama.cpp" is the software running ON that
   GPU, not an alternative to it — `LlamaCppClient` IS the GPU client. Every
   measurement since ADR-0059 went to this server; the only Groq-based
   measurement in the project's history is the 2026-08-13 scoring probe, long
   superseded. Say "servidor GPU" for the provider; say "llama.cpp" only when
   its internals (slots, prompt cache, grammar) are the subject. Server-specific
   request parameters (`id_slot`, `cache_prompt`, …) are not to be used in
   production or diagnostics unless the user explicitly asks; the gpt-oss
   `reasoning_effort` level is a MODEL setting, not a server internal.
7. **Reproducibility** is the core academic constraint (professor's requirement).
   BM25 (deterministic retrieval) + temperature=0 = same input → same score.
8. **Scope: all 46 Ações.** Phase 1 began with Ação 1 only. Since 2026-09-07 every
   Ação has its IPMP file and retrieval profile, and every assessment evaluates all
   46 (the scope comes from the IPMP store, ADR-0030). Every measurement so far uses
   one case (SMG-040). On 2026-10-16 the user accepted the stability margin and closed
   the scorer verification, the condition set for a second case document; the
   document is the user's to supply.
9. **IPMP ingestion: manual.** PDF structure is mixed text/image; data is static.
   Expected products sub-items (a/b/c/d) per action become BM25 search queries.
10. **Development methodology:** CONTEXT.md → ADR → to-PRD → grill-me per module.
    **Deep modules:** one module fully designed and built before the next begins.
    No stubs, no parallel tracks, no "fill in later."

---

## Auditor Review Interface (7 elements)

For each scored action, the system presents to the human auditor:
1. IPMP criteria for the action
2. Retrieval query used
3. Retrieved chunks (with source document + page number)
4. Exact LLM prompt sent
5. LLM reasoning
6. Uncertainty flag
7. Proposed score (0 / 1 / 3)

## ⏭️ Where to Resume

**Session 2026-10-19 (81st pass) — THE USER'S REQUESTS AJ–AK: close the frontend for testing the stored case through the current UI, then *"O botão de marcar como relevante está lá, mas como fazer para o sistema reavaliar aquele caso? Após, deve ser possível aplicar novo score para aquela ação. Justificativa é importante."* and the answers *"O auditor pode ver todos e reexecutar no final ou reexecutar todos, sob sua conta e risco"*, reviewed Ação *"apagar com confirmação"*, no justification on accept, *"recalcula as dependentes"*, *"Pode commitar"*. Re-evaluation of selected Ações is built (ADR-0078). Two commits; nothing pushed.**

- **Commit `176ec3c`:** `main.py` defaults `LLAMACPP_BASE_URL` to the tunnel (`http://127.0.0.1:18083`); UploadView opens a stored case and replaces an assessment (force, with a confirmation, ADR-0026); AcaoPanel gains the "Fora por gênero" column and drops the alert asking for a per-Ação re-run that did not exist.
- **Re-evaluation (ADR-0078):**
  - `POST /api/cases/{p}/reevaluate {acao_ids, delete_reviews}`: retrieval, genre filter, gate and scorer again for each listed Ação from the indexed documents; the ledger replays every recorded verdict and override, only unseen chunks reach the model. 422 unknown Ação or empty list, 404 no evaluation, 409 with the reviewed targets and reviewed dependents until `delete_reviews=true`, 503 server down (nothing changed) or lost midway (report with `not_reached`).
  - Dependents: one ascending walk; an Ação citing with effect (ADR-0070) an Ação whose score changed gets `recompute_dependency_cap` on its stored result (no model, row keeps its `created_at`); its review is deleted only if its score changed, and its own dependents follow.
  - `GET /api/cases/{p}/reevaluation/pending`: overrides recorded after the evaluation of their Ação.
  - UI: a "Reavaliação" block on the case page ("Reavaliar Ações com override pendente", "Reavaliar todas as Ações" with its own confirmation, progress, report), a chip "Override pendente — reavaliar" on each pending Ação; the 409 becomes a confirmation listing the reviews to be deleted. A new score comes through the existing review (accept without a note, override with a justification, ADR-0023).
  - Code: `src/evaluation/dependency_cap.py` (`recompute_dependency_cap`), `src/assessment/service.py` (`run_reevaluation`, `pending_gate_overrides`, `reviews_at_stake`), `routes.py`, `schemas.py` (`ReevaluationRequest`), `AssessmentResultView.vue`, `AcaoPanel.vue`. CONTEXT.md: Reavaliação, Override Pendente; ledger and cap entries amended.
- **Checks:** `tests/test_reevaluation.py` (13); fast suite **770**. Headless Chrome on a copy of `data/app.db` with stub gate and scorer: the pending path re-evaluated Ação 17 (sem evidência → 1 on the auditor's `_2` p.160 for 17a), emptied the pending list, kept the 45/46 reviews, 2 gate misses; the all path showed both confirmations, deleted the 45/46 reviews on the copy, 28 gate misses (narrative sections and cleaner stamp text since 2026-10-16). No overflow at 400 px. `data/app.db` untouched.
- **State left:** the model, tunnel and app are down (the user authorized it). The user's test override (Ação 17, 17a, `_2` p.160, justification "Teste") is in the real ledger and will show as pending; revert it with an override "não relevante" before re-evaluating if it was only a test. To use re-evaluation: `gpu_livre.ps1`, `sobe_8083.ps1`, tunnel, app.
- **Known limits:** the scorer is not ledgered, so re-evaluating an unchanged batch can still move a score (about 9 %); an override in the same second as its evaluation is not listed as pending.

**Session 2026-10-19 (80th pass) — THE USER'S REQUESTS AG–AI: cycle 11 (variant S: narrative sections of instrument documents reach the gate), then "para o universo de um processo ... vale a pena manter a lógica de S", then "Retire cronograma da lista e pode implementar S no código com o ADR". S is IN `src/` without CRONOGRAMA (ADR-0077). Uncommitted until the user's OK.**

- **Cycle 11** (`--narrative-sections`, one ten-process invocation, 10:03–12:32; criterion `scratchpad/acc/criterio_ciclo11.txt`): A passed (40/46, as C8); B failed (1 of 6 targets: TR objectives `_1` p.140 for 1c in 10/10; the four 4a targets coins at 2/10); C1/C2 passed (0 confusions in 5+, no modal change, all 17 pre-listed confusions rejected 10/10); C3: 4 new coins, the schedule annex `_2` p.974 once for 31.1f (target confusion with 31.1c, one point to Ação 31 in process 6). Process 9 lost Ação 23 to gate timeouts, not refilled. Readers: session scratchpad `c11/analisa_ciclo11.py`, `c11/leitor_c11.py`.

New rule for evidence-recovery changes: non-inferiority (A, C1, C2) plus one legitimate target (ADR-0077).
- **Code:** `src/retrieval/genre/narrative.py`; `chunk_genre.narrative_section` (an old-shape table is dropped and rebuilt, derived data); `get_chunk_genres(..., release_narrative_sections=True)` reads released chunks as OUTRO; `get_narrative_sections`; `GENRE_RULES_VERSION` 2026-10-19.1; `evidence_selection.NARRATIVE_SECTIONS_ENABLED`; harness `--no-narrative-sections` (`--narrative-sections` stays as the cycle-11 record). `tests/test_genre_narrative.py` (26); fast suite 757.
- **Checked without a model** (session scratchpad `c12/confere_s_src.py`, `c12/projeta_s_src.py`): 30 released chunks = cycle 11's 33 minus the schedule annex; examined pools = cycle 11's except `_2` p.973/974 leave 20d, 31.1f, 35.2b, 45; 473 pool calls per process. `data/app.db`'s index rebuilds on its next read.
- **Infra, disclosed:** model started 10:02 after `gpu_livre.ps1` LIVRE, stopped 12:35 (`para_8083.ps1` via `ssh_ps_file.py`); `infra_c11.py` kept the tunnel and read the GPU every 5 min (all LIVRE).

**Session 2026-10-18/19 (79th pass) — THE USER'S REQUESTS AD–AF: items 1–4 on instrument text (which products may read it, a second look at 12 13 15 24 35 36 42, the window depth, the gate judging sufficiency), then "Um ciclo para a variante estreita do gate", then "Vamos aplicar o filtro conforme sua sugestão e analisar o resultado". Two variants measured, each as ONE ten-process invocation of the harness under a criterion written before the first model call; both FAILED and are discarded, kept in the harness as the record. Recorded in ADR-0074 ("Variants tried after cycle 8"). `src/` unchanged.**

- **Standing rule (the user, verbatim: "Isso deve ser regra"):** never give instrument text back to the gate as a general rule, never take query terms from this case, never write negative patterns tailored to its pages. Item 1 found no product with a canonical reason to read instrument text.
- **Correction:** the justification `_1` p.3 is partial legitimate evidence for 13c, 2c and 14a (13a does not reach it), debatable for 15a, 15c, 35.2e; it had been recorded as false for 13c. The filter does not remove it (genre OUTRO); the gate rejects it on sufficiency.

    - drop the empty-product clause from the uncertainty rule;
    - fix "acima" and "licitário";
    - reword the summary line;
    - capture the analysis channel;
    - a three-state verdict (changes ADR-0058).
    Editing IPMP texts also changes gate prompts, while builder changes touch only the scorer. Any change to all scorer prompts is judged by full-process cycles.
- **M1, today's scorer on fixed batches** (`scripts/diagnostics/measure_scorer_reasoning.py`, new):
  - **Run:** 20 batches × 10 passes = 200 calls, 0 errors, 01:41–02:11. Outputs `scratchpad/acc/scorer_raciocinio.{json,log}`; readers `$W/analisa_scorer_raciocinio.py` and `$W/duvidas_scorer.py` → `$W/acc_scorer_raciocinio.txt`.
  - **Setup:** the gate is not called. The stored gate verdicts of a C6/C7 process are replayed inside `select_evidence`, and the rebuilt batch equals the stored one (20/20). The production prompt is byte-identical in every pass.
  - **Batches:** 13 consistent, 4 tolerable, 3 inconsistent (A13 4, A37-C7r6 4, A46 3); 18 changes against the mode.
  - **Justifications:** read one by one, 7 of the 8 varying products have the same reading in all 10 passes. What varies is the translation of "parcialmente atendido" into the boolean `evidenced`. 4 calls contradict their own justification (26a ×2, 37, 10a).
  - **Uncertainty:** true in 150 of 200 calls, and 75 analyses tie it to an empty product (F4). 0 of 200 reasonings cite another Ação, and the analysis never mentions the Exceções section.
  - **32a:** the concessionaire's operating-staff cost table is credited in 10/10, and no call asks whose costs they are.
  - **Classification** (the agreed plan's question): criterion ambiguity in the output contract, not reading noise.
- **M2, two candidates against today's prompt** (`scripts/diagnostics/measure_scorer_candidates.py`, new):
  - **Run:** arms atual, C and CH on the same 20 batches, 10 passes, the (batch, arm) order shuffled in each pass. 600 calls, 02:14–03:47, 1 timeout. The criterion was written before the first call (`scratchpad/acc/criterio_candidatos_scorer.txt`). Outputs `scratchpad/acc/scorer_candidatos.{json,log}`; readers `$W/analisa_candidatos.py` → `$W/acc_candidatos_final.txt`, `$W/duvidas_candidatos.py`, `$W/compara_sessoes_scorer.py`, `$W/le_justificativas_m2.py`, `$W/le_inversoes_m2.py`.
  - **C:** the Guide §4.1 three levels per product. The schema field is `nivel` (atendido / parcial / nao_atendido), the justification comes before it, and each level is defined. The count gives 3 if all products are atendido, 1 if any is atendido or parcial, else 0. F4–F7, F9 and F10 are fixed.
  - **CH:** C plus the non-scored header repeated in the title of each fragment block (D1).
  - **Results (atual / C / CH):**
    - batches consistent 11/15/16, tolerable 7/2/2, inconsistent 2/3/2;
    - changes against the mode 16/14/12; points outside the mode 28/17/14;
    - right outcome on the 14 batches with a fixed reading 103/94/94 of 140;
    - uncertainty 155 of 199 / 27 / 22 of 200;
    - field against its own justification 3/0/0 (read by hand); the model's own score ≠ the counted one 16/0/0;
    - tokens per call 854 / 1 061 / 1 126.
  - **Rules A–E:** A fails for both (the inconsistent batches are not fewer). B fails for both (94 < 103). C passes for both. D is reported: 3a stays 3, the three 37 batches move to mode 1 (the Guide's "parcial"), and 10a r5 moves from mode 0 to 1. E: CH is not preferred over C, being identical on the scope batches. **Neither candidate is adopted.**
  - **What three levels fix:** the translation doubt. 13c, 2c, 32a, 26a and 10a r10 are constant 10/10; contradictions go 3 → 0; the model's score equals the counted one in all 400 candidate calls; uncertainty tied to an empty product goes 71 → 4 and 2.
  - **What they do not fix:** the gate's wrong-scope confusions (32a, 13c, 35.2e, 42d) are credited "parcial" in every pass.
    - Today's binary credits them most of the time and refuses by chance, and rule B counts those refusals as right.
    - No call in any arm asks whose team 32a's costs belong to.
    - The profiles' negative patterns, which only the gate reads, do not name these classes (13c has none).
    - The scope sits in the IPMP's `por_que_importante` (for 32: "recursos de suporte à gestão do projeto"), which the scorer never receives.
  - **Where the doubt now sits:** the facts are the same in every pass, and the level oscillates between neighbours.
    - 46.1 (6 parcial, 4 nao_atendido): a risk-matrix line telling the signing to follow due process. Does a rule about an act record that it happened?
    - 37, a single product: how much of the set is enough. A level step is now worth 1 or 2 points, not 3.
    - The hesitation index of the analysis channel does not fall.
  - **Session effect in the scorer:** today's prompt is byte-identical in M1 and M2 (20/20 hash pairs).
    - Yet 46.1 was credited 7/10 in M1 and 1/10 in M2 (37 r6: 6 vs 4; 13c: 6 vs 7), so a rate from one session is not the batch's rate.
    - Pooled, today's arm is right in 200 of 280 calls (71 %), against 94 of 140 (67 %) for C and CH.
    - By mode (not pre-registered): atual 10 of 14 batches in M2 and 9 in M1, C and CH 9. The only batch lost is 46.1.

- **Page:** https://claude.ai/artifact/1Hn43gG8baHoZpdojDKxuP (private; built by `$W/gera_pagina_scorer.py` from `$W/sintese_m2.json`).
- **Message Y (verbatim):** *"Adotar C ou CH agora? não. / Medir C2? C2 é C mais o "Por que é importante" e o "Momento ideal". No entanto, o "momento ideal" é abstrato para o scorer. A importância é um conceito estático daquele produto, mas o momento não é capturado só com conceito, pois é temporal e procedimental. Para usar momento ideal é preciso cercar o prompt com ideais que o ambientem naquela ação. Se isso for possível, mantemos e testamos, se isso não for possível, avalie retirar do prompt para evitar halucinação por prover informação que não apresenta contexto. / Tempestividade. A definição de Atendido do Guia pede evidência "clara, completa e tempestiva"; C deixou "tempestiva" de fora, mas incluir tempestiva requer um contexto similar ao tópico anterior. Se eu digo que é tempestiva, tenho que fornecer parâmetros que a LLM possa trabalhar para identificar essa tempestividae, caso contrário o retorno será o que a LLM achar adequado, que simplificadamente é um tiro no escuro, pode me dar qualquer resultado. / Registro do scorer. Continua adiado. / Telemetria (F8). Os JSON de ciclo não guardam justificativa nem reasoning, e o cliente descarta o canal de análise. Guardá-los no harness de reprodutibilidade evita um harness à parte em cada verificação como esta. / Itens que independem disto: o commit (CLAUDE.md e os dois scripts de medição); parar a tarefa 8083 (PID 24416); um /assess novo; o rótulo "Inicial"; N/A para 45/46; a promoção dos perfis seed; a linha de assinatura que o removedor de ruído deixa passar."*
- **Reading of Y:**
  - C and CH are not adopted.
  - C2 = C + "Por que é importante". The "Momento ideal" enters only if it can be situated in the Ação; if not, it leaves, so as not to give the scorer information without context.
  - "tempestiva" enters only with parameters the model can work with.
  - The scorer ledger stays deferred.
  - F8 is read as approved, in the diagnostics harness only.
  - The independent items were listed without a decision; none was acted on.
- **Momento and tempestividade, evaluated:**
  - The Guide defines tempestividade (§5.1.1, p. 196; FAQ 6, p. 239): a document counts only if it was produced before the decision it supports or before the contracting, and "estudos bem estruturados, mas intempestivos" do not fulfil the action. Applying it needs each document's production date and the decision's date.
  - The scorer sees only `[Arquivo | Página]` per passage. The pipeline extracts no document date, and the document index (ADR-0074) stores boundaries, title and genre, not dates. So the scorer cannot test tempestividade, and "tempestiva" stays out of every arm. A later route: extract document dates into the document index and show them in the passage header (a separate change).
  - `momento_ideal`: 17 Ações (1–5, 11–17) share "Fase inicial de estudo de viabilidade do empreendimento." and 9 (29–36, 38) share "Após a definição da melhor alternativa e antes da licitação."; the others name a milestone or another Ação.
  - The Ação can be situated with canonical data: its fase and block (1–20, 21–37, 38–46), the five transition points, and the typed edges of `dependencias.json` with the titles of the related Ações. The passages cannot be situated. So the momento enters only as context, with a usage rule (the passages carry no date; no level is granted or denied on an assumption about when a document was produced), and only in a separate arm, C2M, so that a hallucination would show against C2.

Runner `$W/scorer_c2_runner.py`, started 11:31 with the GPU free (0 %); outputs `scratchpad/acc/scorer_c2.{json,log}`; reader `$W/analisa_c2.py` (it reproduces M2's totals exactly) → `$W/acc_m3_final.txt`; hand reading `$W/le_m3.py` → `$W/acc_m3_leitura.txt`.

E decides the momento: C2M must not be worse than C2 on changes, inconsistent batches, right calls and right modes, and no product may fall from atendido or parcial to nao_atendido on timing-based justifications.

There the atual gets 8 of 40 calls right by refusing at random, and the candidates none ("parcial" in every pass). On the other 10 fixed batches C2M gets 99 of 100 right and the atual 94; the difference is 46.1.

      - Scale (consistent/tolerable/inconsistent): 38/4/4 → 34/8/4.
      - Identical per pair: batch 36.5 → 35.1, bits 40.9 → 39.6, points 43.0 → 42.3. In points, the contributions of Ações 23 and 32 cancel exactly.
      - Identical in all ten: batch 31 → 26, bits 34 → 30.
      - Coins: 65 → 70.
      - Point changes: 136 → 166 (gate 139; scorer 18, all in Ação 10; cap 9, Ação 3 via Ação 2).
      - ADR-0068 signal: fired for 9 of the 12 varying Ações; misses 3, 10 and 37.
    - **Noise floor, measured:**
      - Ações 1–22 are gated before any changed prompt, so for them the two cycles are twenty processes of one configuration:
        - 23 coin pairs in each cycle, but on different pairs (Ação 22: 6 → 2; Ação 2: 6 → 8);
        - same points in all ten: 19 → 17 (13 and 14 moved once each);
        - same points in 9+ of 10: 21 = 21.
      - After Ação 23, Ações with unchanged prompts moved both ways: 35 and 37 better; 40, 42 and 45 worse.
      - So two 10-process cycles resolve a one-Ação change only where the change touches that Ação, and "identical in all ten" moves by about two by chance.
    - **Outcome: neither pattern is written.**
      - The rule names a constant accept turned into a coin as the loss; both patterns did that.
      - 32a also lowers the constancy of its Ação.
      - 23.3 raises Ação 23 in points only because two coins rarely land together, and it switches off P1 on p.144.
      - Read in points alone, 23.3 would pass for Ação 23; this is recorded for the user.
      - The result is in ADR-0074 ("Test cycle 4") and in the docstring of the candidates file. The baseline stays `5be6d44`.
    - **Round-2 draft** (`$W/candidatos_rodada2_23_32.py`; NOT measured, NOT applied; overfitting audit clean):
      - It uses markers the passage itself decides: for 23.3, whether the recipient of the value is the public authority; for 32a, whether the staff's function is to operate or maintain the service.
      - It cannot keep p.144, which arrives only as a neighbour of p.145.

**What was done:**

- **Fast suite:** 636 → 691.
- **Production code vs the A2D projection:** reproduced exactly, with no GPU.
- **GPU server:** the task was down after the owner's reboots and was started over SSH.

**Code (nothing committed):**
- **`src/retrieval/genre/`:**
  - `classifier.py` — port of the v3+v4 prototype, with identical labels on 1 429/1 429 chunks. The act dateline that named the case's city now matches any "Cidade, d de mês" (still 84 marks on SMG-040).
  - `segmentation.py` — port of `segmenta_docs.py`.
  - `index.py` — three tables in the retrieval DB: `document_index`, `chunk_genre` and `document_index_state`.
    - Rebuilt on read when the chunk fingerprint changes (count + sha256 over keys and lengths) or when `GENRE_RULES_VERSION = "2026-10-13.1"` changes.
    - Dropped explicitly by `invalidate_document_index`, called for new or replaced files (`assessment.service._process_file`) and on force-delete (`routes._force_delete`).
  - Exported through `retrieval` as `get_chunk_genres`, `get_document_index` and `invalidate_document_index`.

- **`src/evaluation/evidence_selection.py`:**
  - The genre filter runs before `_prefilter_near_duplicates`, which runs before the cut of 16.
  - `EXPANSION_ENABLED = True`. Instrument neighbours are never gated, for any product, exempt ones included; already-judged neighbours are skipped (`seen` / `skip_keys`).
  - `_EVIDENCE_BUDGET_CHARS` 40 000 → 70 000.
  - New `filter_focal_document`. With no instrument chunk, or every product exempt, the document stays whole. With mixed exemption, the route falls through to the hybrid route. With none exempt, instrument chunks are dropped, and an empty result also falls through.
  - `GENRE_FILTER_ENABLED` switches it all off.
- **Telemetry:** `ProductGateReport.n_genre_excluded` and `n_neighbors_genre_excluded`.
- **`reproducibility.py`:**
  - new switches `--no-genre-filter` and `--no-expansion` (`--expansion` kept);
  - the JSON records `expansion`, `genre_filter` and `genre_rules_version`;
  - the per-product vector has 8 elements (the last two are the genre counters).
- **Noise stripping:** `strip_extraction_noise` moved to `src/extraction/noise.py`. Its header and signature patterns are still the Rio SMG ones, a pre-existing limitation recorded in ADR-0074.

  - new expansion, filter and focal tests in `test_evidence_selection.py`;
  - two service tests for invalidation;
  - `conftest.py` stubs genres and neighbours for every test.

- **Classifier** (`$W/tipo_texto4.py` over `$W/tipo_texto3.py`). Each chunk is labelled INSTRUMENTO (contract or minuta, edital, TR, normative annexes) or OUTRO (study, ETP, opinion, act, justification).
  - Instrument markers are general drafting conventions of Brazilian procurement: self-reference ("deste CONTRATO"), a defined party in CAPS with a deontic or future verb, impersonal prescriptive voice, enumerated infinitive obligations, numbered clauses, quoted definitions, form placeholders, risk-matrix rows and risk allocated to a party, bidding-procedure terms, sanction grades, clause cross-references, semicolon lists.
  - Counter-markers: the analysis voice (estimou-se, parecer, premissas, fluxo de caixa, VPL…) and the administrative-act voice (DESPACHO, AUTORIZO, Certifico…).
  - Short silent chunks inherit from chunk 0 of the same page or the ±2-page firm majority. Doubt → OUTRO. Corpus: 1 103 instrument, 326 other.
  - Evaluated three ways, none used to set a rule page by page. Against the process's own document structure (SIGA authentication codes, evaluation only): edital, minutas, matrices and forms 362/365; studies, ETP, opinions, justification and acts 0/150 marked instrument. TRs 34 instrument / 28 other: descriptive sections (context, history, justification) stay by design; equipment drawings and design-requirement lists partly stay. The cycle-2 class map: 114/120 and 17/18. A blind sample read by hand before comparing: 56/58 and 2/2.
- **Pools** (`$W/lanes_profundas.py` deep lanes, verified to reproduce today's lists; `$W/pools_rapido.py` → `scratchpad/acc/pools_filtrados.json`). 1 807 of the 2 045 examined candidates of the 147 non-exempt products are instrument text (88 %).

- **Canonicity** (CONTEXT.md Provenance; ADR-0073 clarification; `docs/prompts/04` and `05`). A canonical term is general vocabulary of the product's IPMP concept, or of the legislation or official manual that concept relies on, valid for any document of any type. IPMP-literal is sufficient, not necessary. Sector vocabulary becomes `synthetic`/experimental (ADR-0056). Tailored terms are removed.
- **Why 35.2f was applied and 35.2e rejected (the user's question).** Same rule at the time: direction in-process, then constancy across processes. 35.2e failed in-process (`_2` p.776 FTTTT→TTTTT), so it never reached the cross-process stage. 35.2f passed both stages (p.126 TFTTT→FFFFF across processes).
- **35.2e across processes** (`$W/a35e_runner.py` → `scratchpad/acc/a35e_{cur,cand}{1..5}`; pools identical; ADR-0072 new section; `candidates_acao35_round5.py` docstring). p.776 went from 7/10 accepted to 1/5 — the in-process verdict was wrong about direction. But Ação 35's outcomes went from 1,1,sem,1,1 to sem,sem,1,0,1. Changes against the mode went 1/5 → 3/5, and pairs with equal score 60 % → 20 %. Batch constancy and verdict constancy were unchanged. ADR-0068 flags went 2/5 → 4/5. **NOT adopted.**
  - **Mechanism (general, binding for future reading): the coin-removal path.** When a positive score rests on several false coins, removing the most frequent coin hands the score to rarer coins. The outcome gets LESS constant while the target moves the right way. The score is constant only at the two ends: enough coins that one always lands (false credit), or none left (a stable not-met).
  - Under today's rule 35.2f would not qualify either. Over 10 current processes it gives 1 ×7, 0 ×2 and sem ×1.

- **Method finding (no GPU, from the stored harness reasoning, `$W/padrao_passadas.py`)**: inside one harness process the five passes are mostly the SAME answer replayed — of 458 trajectories with reasoning, **385 (84 %) have five byte-identical reasonings**, 53 (12 %) differ only in pass 1, 20 (4 %) otherwise; over 696 trajectories 99.1 % are constant. A 5-pass in-process measurement is one or two observations per chunk, not five — the mechanism behind ADR-0071's "within-process is not run-to-run": the verdict of a borderline prompt is a deterministic function of the prompt AND the server state (prefix reuse/slot), and the cross-process coin is that state. The one experiment that could turn coins into constants — cross-process constancy with the computation made independent of server state — needs server-internal request parameters, used only if the user asks (standing rule).

- **ADR-0069, applied and measured** (three independent clean passes, ledger off): neighbour expansion **off by default** (`EXPANSION_ENABLED = False` in `evidence_selection.py`, switchable via `reproducibility.py --expansion`), the anchor cap made **positional** (the gate walks the whole examined pool instead of stopping at the 5th accept), the 0.70 semantic dedup made **per Expected Product**, `_MAX_EVIDENCE_CHARS` 40 000 -> 70 000 re-derived from the server's own 32 000-token slot, and truncation made **round-robin over products**. Final state over three passes (34.7/35.2/36.2 min): leva identical 24/46, bits 34/46, score 38/46, 8 Ações with variable score. **100 % of the residual differences are the gate answering a byte-identical prompt differently** — 78 (chunk, product) pairs in 41 of 164 products over 65 pages.
- **The 78 pairs, read one by one and grouped by CLASS OF PAGE** (`scratchpad/classifica.py`, `acc/instaveis.txt`): MATRIZ 17 pairs/9 chunks (Ações 12 13 15 28 35 42 43), EDITAL 14, OBRIG 9, OBJETO 8, PARECER 7, REEQ 7, GARANT 5, OPER 4, ESTRAT 3, ESPEC 3, CHECK 1. **63 of 78 are a confound; 15 are the gate hesitating on a legitimate target.** 22 of the 49 (class, product) cells have no negative pattern at all; 27 have one that does not catch the class. **The finding that decides where a fix may live: the same page is the legitimate target of some products and a confound of others** — the risk-matrix row is the literal evidence of 8d/23.4/22.2 and noise for 11 products across 7 Ações; the procedural edital is the target of 25b/25c/25d and noise for 5. A global prompt rule would delete 5 true bits to buy 63 false ones, so the fix is **per product, in the retrieval profile or in the IPMP text**, never in the builder. **Only 12 of the 78 pairs sit inside an Ação whose score actually varies** (8 24 27 31 33 37 42 45; Ação 24 is scorer-only), across just 9 products — all of them Ações at band margin 1 (ADR-0068).
- **IPMP SWEEP (user instruction: "varredura no documento IPMP ... conferir uma a um se os dados do IPMP estão adequadamente populados nos perfis, corrigindo, melhorando e informando")**. The 46 Ações were extracted straight from `docs/IPMP TCU2026 - Indicador_percepcao_maturidade.pdf` (246 pages, text-extractable; `scratchpad/ipmp_pdf.py`) and compared field by field. **Transcription is faithful 46/46** — 7 blocks each, 164 scored products, 138 worked examples; the single flagged product (11b, ratio 78) is an improvement, not a defect: the transcriber inlined footnote 55. Profile structure is clean: **0 scored products without a profile entry, 0 orphan entries**.
- **Corrections applied, 14 files.** (1) **Nine IPMP footnotes referenced inside criterion sections had never reached the data**, three of them defining the very term the criterion uses — inlined in parentheses at the exact field the PDF anchors them to, following 11b's convention: **5b** <- note 52 (definition of *risco sistêmico*, AACE 10S-90 — settles the 4b/5b/27c collision at the source and separates it from 27c's "riscos sistemáticos"); **Ação 8** descricao_acao <- 53 ("há de se ter o registro formal dos fundamentos"; definitive modelling belongs to Ação 22) and o_que_esperar <- 54; **Ação 15** <- 56 (Acórdão 1182/2025-TCU-Plenário); **16b** <- 57 (control body = TCU, other Courts of Accounts or the internal-control network — **the asymmetry with 11b is now fixed**); **18b** <- 59 (definition of *viés do otimismo*, Kahneman); **23.4** <- 65 (Dispute Boards Permanentes e Vinculantes); **Ação 28** <- 66 (the year-by-year test considers the body's other disbursements and its history — the "conta corrente"); **Ação 36** excecoes <- 68 (the independent third party may be a consultancy or a body such as the BNDES). (2) **The functional defect of the sweep — Ação 1 / 1c**: the words "risco", "matriz" and "alocação" appear in **no field of Ação 1** (title, description, o_que_esperar, products, exceptions, all three examples), yet 1c carried a concept `risk_framework` ("Matriz de riscos e alocação de responsabilidades entre as partes") — which goes into the gate prompt as a sub-criterion — plus two active terms ("matriz de risco", `NEAR(alocação,risco)`) and a logic pattern declaring the contractual matrix valid evidence, while 1a of the same Ação carries a negative pattern excluding exactly that content. **Removed**; 1c had 2 unstable pairs. (3) **Two real coverage gaps**: **1d** named only municipal instruments although the criterion names six federal ones (PNL, PDE, Plansab, PNLP, Plano Aeroviário Nacional, PNH) and five levels — new concept `federal_sectoral_planning_instruments`, 7 active terms + 4 experimental acronyms (PDE collides with Plano Diretor Estratégico, with its own negative pattern); **15e** was missing the criterion's own purpose clause (confirm whether the risks can be transferred to the market, making the project viable or not). (4) **Provenance: 23 terms re-labelled `canonical` -> `real_world`** — marked as IPMP-derived but absent from the Ação's text and literally present in the case corpus (Ações 1, 3, 4, 5, 28, which predate the synthesis process). The label is what makes overfitting auditable; it changes neither prompt nor retrieval (status governs the search). 13 further canonical terms with no support in the IPMP **or** the corpus are flagged and left untouched: calling them `synthetic` would, per ADR-0056, imply demoting them to experimental, which is a retrieval change and must be measured, not slipped in.
- **Finding for the record, no change made: `evidence_logic_patterns` is DORMANT** — 225 patterns across the 164 products, read by no consumer, exactly as `negative_evidence_patterns` was before 2026-08-27. Earlier passes wrote those patterns as if they steered the gate. Practical consequence for item 4: a candidate must go into `evidence_intent`, the concepts or the negative patterns — the fields that are actually read.
- **Routine check added**: `audit_gate_prompts.py` now reports **corpus leakage in canonical fields** (document name, page, validation date, file, lot) — regex, local, with a negative test; **zero in 164 products**, and the nine inserted footnotes (Lei 14.133/2021, art. 169, Acórdão 1182/2025, AACE 10S-90) do not trip it.
- **Validation of the sweep**: stores reload at 46 Ações / 164 products; gate-prompt audit 164/164 correct, digest `6c059ec7703bb439` -> **`d25899dbb729c620`** (expected: 7 products changed); concept drift 4 -> 3 (the remaining 3 are Ação 1's legitimate operationalisations of a vague criterion); **fast suite 587 passing**.
- **ITEM 4 — the nine candidates are written and structurally verified; the GPU measurement is BLOCKED.** New `scripts/diagnostics/measure_criterion_candidates.py` (versioned) holds each candidate with its direction, patch, IPMP citation, targets and control set, patches the profile **in memory only**, and reuses `audit_gate_flips.build_targets(acao, products={pid})` so the prompt is the production one. Because each Expected Product has its own prompt, **a candidate can only move that product's verdicts** — the risk of every change is structurally bounded to one product and the control set is the rest of its examined pool. Firm-accept candidates: **8d** (the matrix row *is* "levantamento de riscos contratuais"; maturity is the scorer's judgement, not a relevance condition), **8b** (note 53's formal record of the grounds), **37** (what counts as internal approval: conformity attestation, internal opinion, dated approval — against the Não Atendido's "versões preliminares"), **45** (the record of the acts, including a later instrument that attests homologação/adjudicação — the Não Atendido turns on the *absence* of formal acts). Firm-reject candidates: **27c** (a clause providing for future updates is not a stress scenario), **27d** (an operational execution duty is not schedule compatibilisation), **31.1g** (a product specification is not a quality standard per stage), **33b** (a standing contractual duty is not an engagement plan), **42c** (a clause providing for future rebalancing is not the analysis of an adjustment already made). **Dry check (73 s, no GPU): every target is inside `ranked[:16]`, none out of pool**; pools 16/16/16/16/14/16/16/13/10 = 133 chunks, so the real measurement is 133 x 2 conditions x 5 passes = **1 330 serial calls, about 25 min**. Two harness defects of mine were found and fixed in the process: the retrieval was being paid 3x per product (now memoised per Ação — retrieval is deterministic, so memoising changes nothing) and the `--dry-run` block had failed to apply, so the first attempt walked into 600 s timeouts against the dead server; **a `/health` fail-fast was added** (ADR-0059 applied to diagnostics: server down -> explicit error, nothing measured).

Mas antes é importante fixar as decisões a respeito de temas que a princípio seu relatório coloca em dúvida … precisamos setar uma configuração ótima ou mais próxima disso e permanecer com ela até a definição do próximo ciclo de ajustes."* Decisions taken via AskUserQuestion: **scorer `reasoning_effort` = medium** (keep; `low` discarded with data); **no scorer ledger this cycle** (candidate for the next one; auditor relies on the uncertainty flag — 14/14 scorer flips flagged — and on ReviewOutcome); **no commit for now** (the 64-entry queue stays pending; the cycle measures the working tree); **test cycle = production path only, 3 runs** (gate ledger ON in a fresh scratch sqlite, cold + 2 warm; the engine's 90 % criterion is NOT re-measured — the 2026-10-03 10-run result 90.9 % / 34/46 / 27/46 is the baseline).
- **The fixed configuration IS the current working tree** (no code change was needed): gate serial + low (ADR-0065), identity block + sibling contrast (ADR-0064), P1 (ADR-0066), V4, `_MAX_EXAMINED=16`, `_ANCHOR_TARGET=5`, dedup 0.92/0.70, gate ledger ON in production with the corrected key (ADR-0067), scorer at medium with the counted score (ADR-0058), the 46 profiles with the 18 fixes of 2026-10-03. **Frozen until the next adjustment cycle is defined: no prompt, threshold, profile or client change without the user.** Deferred candidates (not to be applied silently): scorer ledger; product id in the criterion header; 2d/p.157 and 28.3/p.136 borderlines; gate residue on 37 and single-product fragility on 45.

Runs 45.4 / 9.0 / 9.7 min; 2 513 gate calls per run; run 1 recorded 2 498 verdicts (15 in-run repeats served by the ledger), **runs 2–3 recorded 0 and the ledger has 0 duplicate chunk+product tuples — the key fix is confirmed in production** (yesterday's run 2 wrote 1 row under the old code); all rows `model = gpt-oss-20b-F16.gguf`. **Evidence sets, explicit rejections and per-product counts identical 46/46 in both warm runs.** Outcomes: run 2 = run 1 in 45/46, run 3 in 41/46; identical in all 3 = 41/46 (89 %), the other 5 (10, 12, 23, 27, 42) at 2/3 — **all scorer**: 6/50 (evidenced Ação × warm run) cells changed a product verdict on a byte-identical prompt and all 6 changed the score (10 `1→0` 10a; 12 `0→1` twice, 12b/12c/12d; 23 `3→1` 23.2; 27 `1→0` 27d; 42 `0→1` 42c); with yesterday's 3/48 → 9 score changes in 98 cells (9 %). Run 1 vs the 10-run mode: 41/46, no unseen outcome; frozen off-mode: 12 `0` (mode 1 ×9 — the warm runs went back to 1, scorer), 23 `3` (1 ×8), 42 `0` (1 ×8), 33 `0`, 28 `1` (5/5 tie). Ação 23 gave `3` in both working-tree cold runs vs 2/10 in the frozen tree: the only mechanical difference is that the ledger answers in-run repeats (23.4 had 4: candidate then neighbour) with the same verdict instead of re-asking — intended, but it moves that Ação's mode. Cold run today vs cold run yesterday (independent ledgers, identical prompts): 90/2 412 verdicts differ (3.7 %; 38 A→R, 52 R→A; 4:13, 15:7, 25:7, 1:6, 12:6, 36:5), outcome same 40/46, final evidence set same 23/46 — what the ledger removes from the 2nd evaluation on. **17 chunks frozen on the minority side** (final-set presence in the 10 runs vs the frozen verdict; `acc/ciclo1_agenda.txt`, `scratchpad/ciclo1_agenda.py`): 10a p.106 A; 23.4 p.382/p.309/p.310 A; 26a p.144 A, **26d `_2` p.136 R (7/10)**; 27d p.884 A; 28.2 p.193 A, 28.3 p.136 A; 33b p.388 A; 37 p.40 R, `_2` p.145 R, `_3` p.2 A, p.3 A; 42c p.105 A, p.196 A, **p.493 R (5/10)** — auditor-review candidates (ADR-0067 override), caveat: presence ≠ verdict frequency (dedup/trim). Analysis fixes this pass: `ledger_analysis.py` anchors run 1 on `max(start file, first row − 1 s)` (the start file predates model loading by ~46 s, which had mis-attributed Ação 46's last 6 rows to run 2) and ignores the new 6th `n_ledger_hits` element in the per-product vector (0 cold / = examined warm). **Agenda proposed (§4 of the cycle doc, `acc/agenda_final.md`; the user chooses, configuration frozen, nothing applied)**: residue map of the 12 Ações below 90 % — scorer-pure 32/24/10, gate-borderline 23/26/27/28/33/42/46, single-product 37/45; 6 of the 12 still `profile_maturity: seed` (24, 26, 27, 28, 37, 46). - **METHOD CORRECTION by the user, now governing**: *"eu acredito que o seu foco esteja erroneamene no tratamento por ação que gera defeito e isso é uma falha de método. Precisamos identificar o comportamento do sistema quando problemas como esses ocorrem. Isso importa por que o próximo arquivo de teste não vai ter necessariamente os mesmos problemas nos mesmos itens."* They also refused to fix a comparison baseline (*"Não temos que melhorar em relação a um resultado. Temos que descobrir o que faz um resultado flipar de um score pra outro"*), set the diagnostic sequence (1: are all answered? 2: of those answered, which change between runs? those are the ones to treat), framed the stakes (*"Essa flipagem em mais de 10% das ações macula completamente a análise final. Ou resolvemos o caso ou damos visibilidade para o usuário/auditor … dando a oportunidade ou a obrigatoriedade de interferir no processo"*), asked whether **borderline cases can be identified in a single pass** so the information could be handed to the scorer, accepted reading **(2c)** for verdict review (invert only where it changes the score AND I disagree), and asked for the next steps **split into small decisions**. Accepted; the per-Ação agenda (§4) is now an inventory, not a route.
- **ANSWER 1 — "todos são respondidos?"** (`scratchpad/diag_gate_coverage.py`, read-only over `acc/ciclo1_runs.json`): **0 gate failures** in 2 513 calls, and `aceitos+rejeitados+falhas == examinados` in 164/164 products — no silent verdict anywhere. But **216 of 2 616 candidates (8.3 %), in 49 of the 164 products, never reach the gate** (`examined_pool = ranked[:_MAX_EXAMINED]`, `_MAX_EXAMINED=16`); 16 products exceed 16 examined because neighbour expansion gates chunks from outside the candidate pool (`evidence_selection.py:546`). The cut is deterministic (retrieval is byte-identical), so it is **coverage, not instability** — a separate question from the 90 % criterion.
- **ANSWER 2 — "quais sofrem alteração?" — the finding of this pass, and it is structural, not per-Ação.** Label = the two independent cold runs of the SAME frozen configuration (today's cycle 1 and 2026-10-03), joined on `(acao_id, product_id, filename, page_number, chunk_index)` — **the ledger key cannot join them**: yesterday's rows were hashed over the served name (`Meme-GPT`), today's over the configured label, which is exactly the defect fixed yesterday (`scratchpad/diag_flippers.py`, `diag_impact.py`). 2 412 verdicts in common, 90 flipped (3.7 %) — **but they are not spread, they are in the accepts**: the 2×2 table is 83 accept/accept, 52 accept→reject, 38 reject→accept, 2 239 reject/reject, so **`P(unstable | ACCEPTED) = 38.5 %` (31.4 % seen from yesterday, so not an artefact of conditioning) against `P(unstable | REJECTED) = 1.7 %`, a 23× risk ratio; of the 173 chunks ever accepted, only 83 = 48 % were accepted by both runs.** The gate's rejection is near-deterministic; **its acceptance is close to a coin, and less than half of an accept survives a second pass.**
- **The channel to the score, measured end to end**: verdict 96.3 % stable (90 flips) → **product `evidenced` bit 151/164 = 92.1 % stable (13 flips)** → evidenced-product set differs in **11 of the 26 evidenced Ações** → final outcome differs in 6/46. Two readings make this general rather than case-specific: **8 of the 13 bit flips come from products whose maximum accept count is 1** (16 such products in this case; half of them flipped) — one verdict decides the bit; and **the 0/1/3 band absorbed 5 of the 11 set differences**, so the metric that matters is not the flip count but the **distance to a band change**. The next case will have different chunks and the same structure.
- **ANSWER 3 — "é possível identificar o limítrofe numa única passada?" YES, and with no new model signal: the DIRECTION of the verdict is the detector.** Flagging every accept marks 135 verdicts (5.6 % of the volume) and catches 58 % of the flips at 38.5 % precision. Weaker secondary single-pass signals: `reasoning` length (within rejections, median 295 chars for flippers vs 192 for stable; best threshold ≥ 350 chars flags 229 rejections at precision 6.1 %, recall 37 % of the reject-side flips) and hedging language (10.5 % vs 2.6 %, a 4× lift). Two passes only detect an ambiguous chunk `2q(1−q)` of the time, so at q≈0.7 the real ambiguous set is **≈ 210 chunks (≈ 9 % of verdicts)**, not 90. **Independent corroboration: ADR-0061 measured 29 vote splits in 592 chunks = 4.9 %; this model predicts ≈ 4.3 % — the same population. ADR-0061 rejected voting as a way to DECIDE (sound: at q≈0.5 the majority of three coins is a coin) and deleted `n_vote_splits` as "voting-only"; it never evaluated the split as a LABEL.**
- **THE FIVE DECISIONS put to the user (§6 of the document), none applied, configuration still frozen.** (1) *unit of uncertainty*: per-verdict confidence (needs a prompt or extra calls) vs **margin per product** (how many accepts hold the bit) vs **margin per Ação** (how many bits must flip to change the band) — recommended **1b+1c**: deterministic, zero cost, no model change, and exactly where the score breaks. (2) *what to do with the margin*: **feeding it to the scorer (the user's own idea) is advised AGAINST** — it conflicts with ADR-0058, which deliberately took the count out of the model's judgement; the scorer is the other residue source (9 score changes in 98 byte-identical cells); and a new field changes every scorer prompt, unfreezing the configuration and invalidating the §3.6 measurements. A third `borderline` state would change ADR-0058's arithmetic. Recommended **2c: a deterministic score interval** reported beside the score ("score 1; would be `sem evidência` if 28.2 or 28.3 had not held, both at margin 1") — same input gives the same score AND the same interval, so the reproducibility constraint holds. (3) *who decides the borderline*: first-verdict-wins plus a visible margin (free) vs **re-asking only the ACCEPTS, 2× = +11 % on the cold run (≈45 → 50 min), paid ONCE per case thanks to the ledger**, as a label with the first verdict still deciding — recommended; majority-decides stays rejected on ADR-0061's math. (4) *auditor review*: the user's (2c) becomes a computable rule — **convoke the auditor for a product at margin 1 whose flip changes the Ação's band**; measured volume is **under 10 human decisions per case**, so a blocking mandatory review is defensible. (5) *the cap of 16* (216 ungated candidates): leave it this cycle — recall, not stability. **Recommended order: 1+2c (no GPU, no unfreeze) → 4 → 3b (the only GPU measurement worth it) → 5.**

- **THE FIVE DECISIONS, AS CLOSED BY THE USER (2026-10-05)** — §7 of the document. **(1) Both margins**: per product (how many accepts hold the `evidenced` bit) and per Ação (how many bits must flip to change the 0/1/3 band) — their words: *"determinísticas, custo zero, não tocam no modelo, e são exatamente onde o score quebra."* **(2) The deterministic score interval** reported beside the score (*"Ação 28: score 1; seria sem evidência se 28.2 ou 28.3 não tivessem se sustentado — ambos com margem 1"*); same input gives the same score AND the same interval; nothing goes to the scorer. **(3) REDIRECTED — see the next bullet.** **(4) Auditor review is OPTIONAL**: the system signals the case and explains what happened in its own process, the auditor decides whether to interfere **with the scorer's result**; *"Nada de atuação após o gate"* — read as: the system takes no action on the margin after the gate (no feed to the scorer, no automatic include/exclude, no re-run), the margin is only displayed, and the single intervention point is human and on the final score (ReviewOutcome). **In consequence ADR-0067's gate-verdict override route is NOT the auditor's tool and leaves the interface design.** **(5) The cap of 16 stays** — it was adopted for quality (better chunks were not being gated under the cap of 8); a future reduction to 10–12 is a candidate for filtering junk chunks.
- **DECISION 3 REDIRECTED — the target is CONSTANCY, not the first pass, and this is now the governing frame.** Verbatim: *"não se trata de melhorar em relação ao primeiro, mas sim de manter o resultado, mesmo que a primeira passada mude, o que importa é que o próximo resultado não seja alterado durante as passadas seguintes"*; and *"o limite não deve considerar congelamento de passada"*. Their scale, to be used as the acceptance rule: **0 changes in 10 passes = consistent; 1 change plus 1 more in another 10 = not consistent but tolerable; MORE THAN 2 IN 10 = inconsistent.** And their diagnosis of the scorer, which the data confirms: *"Nesse último caso não há o que fazer no scorer. Ele sempre vai receber uma leva distinta de dados e naturalmente para cada nova leva, necessariamente informará um score distinto. Antes de chegar ao scorer, precisamos entregar uma leva de prompts que não mude a cada passada e isso … tem relação com a constância que parece estar longe aqui."* **Two consequences, both binding: (a) every constancy measurement from here runs with the gate ledger OFF** — the cycle-1 warm runs agreed 46/46 by construction, not by merit, so measuring stability with the ledger on is circular; **(b) the ledger stays in production as reproducibility of a delivered report, and is never again quoted as a stability number.**
- **THE AMPLIFIERS — why the batch is not constant, measured** (`scratchpad/diag_amplificadores.py`, read-only, no GPU). Retrieval is byte-identical (164/164, 2026-10-02), so `ranked[:16]` is a deterministic set; what is not deterministic is **what gets hung off it**. Two steps in `evidence_selection.py` are conditional on the verdict: **(A)** the `_ANCHOR_TARGET=5` early `break` ([evidence_selection.py:413](src/evaluation/evidence_selection.py#L413)) changes which of the 16 are examined — **active but small: only 13 of 164 products reach 5 accepts** (accept distribution `{0:115, 1:15, 2:13, 3:8, 5:5, 6:2, 7:2, 8:1, 9:1, 10:2}`, i.e. 115 products have no evidence at all in this case); **(B)** neighbour expansion ([evidence_selection.py:549](src/evaluation/evidence_selection.py#L549)) gates `prev`/`next` of each **accepted** chunk, i.e. chunks **outside the 16** — **this is the dominant amplifier: 144 of the 150 (96 %) verdict tuples that exist in only one of the two independent cold runs touch an accepted chunk** (82 of the 86 on today's side are on a page adjacent to an accept). Mechanism in one line: **a flip on an accept changes which chunks are even asked.** Of the 2 562 tuples involved, 2 412 are common, 90 are local flips and **150 exist in one pass only, purely by amplification**. Making the examined set verdict-independent would render it **identical by construction**, reducing each flip from "the batch changes" to "one chunk enters or leaves".
- **VOTING IS NOW CLOSED AGAINST THE USER'S OWN CRITERION, with a number** (same script). ADR-0061 rejected it for accuracy; for *constancy* it fails too. `P(same output in two independent passes)` by number of votes: **q=0.50 → 50.0 % at N=1, 3, 5 and 9 (nothing moves, ever)**; q=0.70 → 58.0 / 66.1 / 72.7 / **82.2 %**; q=0.80 → 68.0 / 81.4 / 89.1 / 96.2 %; q=0.90 → 82.0 / 94.6 / 98.3 / 99.8 %. At q=0.70 even **nine** votes give 82.2 %, i.e. ≈1.8 changes in 10 — failing the ≤2-in-10 bar at 9× the cost. Voting does not deliver constancy for a genuinely ambiguous chunk; do not reopen it.
- **REVISED ORDER (answers the user's *"verifique se as respostas alteram a sua proposta de ordem"*: yes, decision 3 changed the target, so the order changed).** **(1) Margins + deterministic interval + the decision-4 signal** — no GPU, does not unfreeze the configuration. **(2) Measure per-chunk constancy with the LEDGER OFF, 10 passes, over the subpopulation that matters** — the 135 accepts plus the 229 long-reasoning rejections, ≈360 chunks instead of 2 513, so ≈45 min of GPU once — giving each chunk's `q` and its change count against the 0 / 1 / >2-in-10 scale; no code change. **(3) Remove the amplification**: neighbour expansion stops depending on the verdict (expand the first N of the ranking, deterministically) and the 5-anchor break becomes positional — **this is a code change and unfreezes the configuration**, measured before/after. **(4) Reduce the ambiguity itself** where (2) reports more than 2 changes in 10, written as a CLASS of confound, never as an Ação. **(5)** ledger demoted to report reproducibility. **(6)** cap of 16 untouched. **Why (2) before (3), inverting the earlier proposal: the right design for neighbour expansion depends on the `q` of the accepts** — if accepts are mostly stable, expanding from them is defensible and making the loop positional suffices; if they are mostly coins, as the measured 38.5 % suggests, expansion must be verdict-independent, which is a larger change. Measuring first costs 45 minutes and avoids redoing the code change.
- **ITEM 1 OF THE REVISED ORDER — DONE (2026-10-05), no GPU, configuration still frozen — ADR-0068.** Decisions 1 + 2 + the decision-4 signal, implemented as arithmetic over data the pipeline already has: same input → same score AND same margin, so reproducibility is untouched, and **nothing is fed to the scorer** (a margin field in its prompt would put back the count ADR-0058 deliberately removed and change every prompt byte). Code: the 0/1/3 rubric extracted into one source of truth (`score_for_counts()` in `parsing/response.py`, which `_score_from_verdicts` now calls and the margin reads, so **the margin cannot disagree with the score it qualifies**); `ProductMargin` / `ScoreMargin` + `EvaluationResult.score_margin` (defaulted — the read path is `json.loads(raw_json)`, ADR-0031, and does not re-validate, so rows persisted earlier keep serving unchanged); new `src/evaluation/margin.py` (`band_margin()` counts in both directions over the rubric itself; `compute_score_margin()` counts support per product from the chunks the scorer actually saw — post-dedup 0.70, post-`_cap_evidence`; `_score_floor()` **simulates removing the chunks that are the sole support of a fragile product and recounts**, rather than subtracting a number, because `_deduplicate_across_products` merges attributions and one chunk may take several bits with it, and a product that keeps ≥1 accept keeps its verdict); `evaluator.py` hoists `expected_product_ids` above the no-evidence return so **both exits** report a margin, attaches it, and adds one line to the progress report when review is recommended; `AcaoPanel.vue` shows the interval, the band margin, a **“Revisão sugerida (opcional)”** alert carrying the deterministic explanation, and a `Margem` column (chip `1` when fragile) — `npm run build` clean. `fragile` is set at **exactly 1 accept**, the only value where the implication is deterministic (no chunk → empty block → the header instruction defines that as NOT evidenced); at 2 the fall is the scorer's call. **No upward claim** is made: the ceiling is the proposed score, since a new accept appearing depends on the scorer. **Floor `None` ≠ floor 0**: when nothing accepted would remain the Ação comes out `sem evidência`, a different outcome from score 0. Tests: new `tests/test_score_margin.py` (25), fast suite **573 passing** (548 → +25), no regressions.
- **ITEM 1 VALIDATED AGAINST THE REAL CYCLE-1 DATA, no GPU** (`scratchpad/validate_margin.py`, read-only over `acc/ciclo1_runs.json`, cold pass; the count available there is the GATE-side `n_accepted`, not the post-dedup one the scorer saw, so the fragile count is a **lower bound**). 25/46 Ações evidenced; **7 of 34 evidenced bits (21 %) rest on a single accept** (fragile products per Ação `{0: 19, 1: 5, 2: 1}`); **20 of the 25 evidenced Ações sit at band margin 1** — one product verdict moves the band — and 5 at 2. **4 Ações flagged** (floor ≠ score AND a fragile product): **23** `3→1` (two fragile), **28** `1→0`, **32** `1→sem evidência`, **45** `3→sem evidência`. **Precision is perfect: all 4 are inside the 12 below 90 %**, none flagged outside it. **Recall 4/12, and that is structural, not a defect of the signal**: of the 8 missed, 10/12/27/42 flipped on byte-identical prompts (scorer side, where the gate has no part) and 26/33/37/42 have **zero** evidenced bits in the cold pass, so there is no fragile bit to fall — what moves them is a NEW accept appearing, the upward side the margin deliberately does not claim. Ações 22 and 35 each have a fragile product and are **correctly not flagged**: that bit falling would not move the band. **A correction to my own earlier wording**: I had written that Ação 28 would be `sem evidência` if 28.2 or 28.3 had not held — wrong, and the code gets it right: 28.3 keeps its accepted chunk (the scorer judged it insufficient), so the gate is not empty and **the real floor is score 0**. That is exactly the distinction `_score_floor` preserves by simulating removal instead of subtracting a count. Full write-up: §8 of `scratchpad/CICLO1_2026-10-04.md`.
- **ITEM 2 OF THE REVISED ORDER — MEASURED (2026-10-05), on the user's GPU server, brought back up over SSH after a power cut. New `scripts/diagnostics/measure_gate_constancy.py`: 10 passes, 388 tuples, 3 880 calls, 0 gate failures, 59.3 min, gate ledger OFF (asserted at startup), nothing persisted.** Subpopulation taken from cycle 1's own ledger — **147 accepts + 241 rejections with reasoning ≥ 350 chars**, against 2 513 calls for a whole pass — because `P(unstable | ACCEPTED)` was 38.5 % against 1.7 % for rejections. **Fidelity is verified, not argued: each prompt was rebuilt from the profile + IPMP + chunk text and its ledger key recomputed against the one production stored — 388/388 identical**, and since the key is a sha256 of (model, effort, system, user), equal hashes mean the same bytes. Stale `measure_unstable_chunks.py` was deliberately NOT edited (MAX_EXAMINED=8, no `reasoning_effort`, no `product_id=` so it misses ADR-0064's sibling contrast) — per the standing precedent, retired scripts stay as templates. **RESULT, on the user's own 0 / 1-2 / >2-in-10 scale: accepts (147) → 59 consistent (40 %), 33 tolerable (22 %), 55 INCONSISTENT (37 %); 88/147 = 60 % moved at least once, median q 0.80, median q of the movers exactly 0.50. Long-reasoning rejections (241) → 173 / 30 / 38, i.e. 28 % moved.** Overall 156/388 (40 %) moved, 93 (24 %) inconsistent, 232 (60 %) never moved, 65 (17 %) are pure coins (q 0.3–0.7). **This settles the question the revised order left open — "if accepts are mostly stable… making the loop positional suffices; if they are mostly coins, expansion must be verdict-independent": the accepts are coins, so item 3 is the LARGER change, neighbour expansion made verdict-independent, not merely a positional break.** Chain to the batch, over the 104 covered products: accepted SET provably identical in two passes only **49/104 (47 %)**, mean P 69.5 %, but mean P(the `evidenced` BIT repeats) **91.1 %** — the difference is what the 0/1/3 band absorbs. Per Ação (44 with tuples): by batch TEXT 24 inconsistent / 2 tolerable / 18 consistent; by BITS, which is the ceiling item 3 could reach, **15 / 5 / 24**. **So item 3 buys 9 Ações from inconsistent to consistent-or-tolerable on the score-relevant quantity and NO Ação constant text** — it bounds a flip to one chunk instead of letting it cascade; the 15 that stay inconsistent are item 4's. **Where the score actually breaks: of the 15 products whose bit rests on exactly one accept, 7 are firm (q ≥ 0.8), 5 are weak (q < 0.3 — cycle 1 froze the MINORITY side, the "17 minority-side chunks" now quantified), and 3 are literal coins: 33c p.43 (q=0.30), 23.2 p.145 (q=0.40), 28.3 p.136 (q=0.60, 8 changes — the worst chunk in the corpus).** For the 34 multi-accept products the OR genuinely protects (median P(bit on) 1.00, 23/34 ≥ 0.95), so a coin chunk does NOT condemn an Ação — 8 of the 12 below 90 % have one, but so do 11 of the 32 above it; the risk is the INTERSECTION. **The finding that answers the method correction: the ambiguity belongs to a CLASS OF PAGE, not to an Ação — 13 pages are inconsistent for more than one product at once** (`_1` p.196/197/198/240/332/106/83, `_2` p.136/144/145/42/20/495), exactly the "attractor chunk" regions of 2026-09 (the risk matrix at `_1` p.196-199 and its duplicate at `_2` p.20/40-41; the Procuradoria parecer at `_2` p.136/144-145). Item 4 must be written per confound class, never per Ação. Revalidations: **0 gate failures in 3 880 calls** (reconfirms ANSWER 1); the single-pass reasoning-length detector works as a THRESHOLD (≥ 350 chars selects a 28 %-unstable population vs 1.7 % overall, ~16×) but **not as a scale** — inside the long rejections, the shorter half and the longer half are both 28 % unstable. **Caveat stated in full**: the call order here is (ação, product, file, page), not production's RRF order; what is faithful is that the order is identical in all 10 passes, as it is in production, so these numbers measure pass-to-pass variation with the sequence held fixed and are therefore a **floor** — projecting `2q(1−q)` over the measured q gives 20.3 % against the 38.5 % measured between two independent cold runs, and that gap IS the amplification item 3 removes. Artefacts: `scratchpad/acc/constancia_{runs.json,log,analysis.txt}`, `scratchpad/constancia_analysis.py`. **No ADR: a measurement is not a decision — the ADR belongs to item 3, which does not start without the user's explicit OK.** Full write-up: §9 of `scratchpad/CICLO1_2026-10-04.md`. GPU server left UP.

**Session 2026-09-27 (51st pass) — ROOT-CAUSE WORK ON SCORE INSTABILITY. Diagnosed the run-to-run score flipping from data and code only (canonical GPU machine off, no real tests possible), then executed a 3-phase fix: diagnostics harness, GPU-only fail-fast + gate-failure telemetry, and a scorer contract where the 0/1/3 score is COUNTED IN CODE from per-product verdicts. 3 new ADRs (0058/0059/0060). Fast suite 432 → 489, all green. NOTHING COMMITTED.**

- **Trigger**: the user supplied 4 consecutive full 46-Ação runs (R1-R4) in which scores flipped between 0/1/3/— for the same documents, and asked for a diagnosis plus improvement plan **without any real test runs** ("Maquina canonica está desligada... vc vai fazer o seu trabalho sem testes reais, apenas olhando os dados"). The user then annotated the proposed plan item-by-item with guardrails and approved execution. **The stated acceptance criterion is stability, not recall: "Preciso que após 10 runs 90% tenha a mesma saída, seja ela um zero ou três."**
- **User guardrails that shaped the work** (each honoured, each worth carrying forward): (a) *"cuidado para não quebrar o que está funcionando. Não haverá testes reais para sanar isso"* — the 432-test offline suite is the only safety net, so it was run after every phase; (b) *"GPU fora → erro explícito. Nada roda... Fica no projeto para mostrar que foi tentado"* — the fallback chain is unwired, not deleted; (c) *"cuidado para não transformar os termos em um localizador de chunks para apenas um tipo de documento. A busca precisa ser canonica"* — **the planned real_world-term extraction from the SMG-040 corpus was DROPPED entirely as an overfitting risk**; (d) *"Aponte todo para a GPU própria"* — nothing added touches Groq or Ollama.

**Diagnosis (measured, not assumed).** Retrieval was verified fully deterministic (flat LanceDB scan, fixed CPU embedding model, FTS5, RRF; LanceDB/SQLite text hashes identical), so **all observed variance originates in the gate or the scorer**. Two concrete defects were found in the scorer path and one in the gate path:
1. `build_user_prompt` **omitted empty product blocks entirely**, and nothing told the scorer a missing block meant "not evidenced" — so the model applied the 0/1/3 rubric to a silently reduced subset. Ação 31 scored a full unhedged **3 having judged 2 of its 9 products**; Ação 2 scored 0 with 1 of 4. Score bands overlapped almost completely against real product coverage (score=3 seen at 22% coverage, score=1 at 100%).
2. **The rubric count was a model judgement**, not arithmetic — a count over a known list has an exact answer.
3. **A broken gate call was indistinguishable from a substantive rejection** — truncation at `max_tokens`, unparseable JSON and server refusals all just dropped the chunk with nothing recorded. A failure rate that varies between runs varies the evidence set, which varies the score, invisibly. **This is the single most likely cause of the observed flipping**, and it also means the 2026-08-27 "content ambiguity, not hardware" conclusion was reached without the data needed to tell a broken call from a confident rejection.
- **A correction to my own earlier framing, worth remembering**: I first presented the per-product-block fix as also damping instability. Re-measured against the 90% criterion, it does not: only 13/46 Ações need ≥2 product flips to change score, while **33/46 change on a single flip** (19 Ações sit at coverage 0, 4 at full coverage, 6 are single-product and structurally 1-flip-fragile). Tier 1 fixes score *correctness*; the stability lever is eliminating silent gate failures and silent model switching.

**Fase 0 — `scripts/diagnostics/` (new, versioned).** Created because **all 17 `scratchpad/*.py` scripts CLAUDE.md references across earlier passes were lost** — untracked AND un-ignored. `scratchpad/` is now in `.gitignore` with a comment saying why. Contents: `_common.py` (offline HF flags, utf-8 stdout rebind, profile/IPMP loaders), `measure_profile_coverage.py`, `measure_retrieval_lanes.py`, `measure_embedding_window.py`, `reproducibility.py` (**the acceptance test** — N runs, per-Ação modal agreement, target ≥90%; deliberately does not persist), and `BASELINE_2026-09-27.md` recording every baseline number. Key measured findings: **889/1008 (88.2%) active query terms are zero-hit against this corpus**, and `build_bm25_query()`'s fallback branch is **unreachable in production** (reachable only from 11 tests) now that every profile has terms — confirmed at the user's request; **deliberately NOT wired as a zero-hit fallback**, because it would inject low-precision noise into the 109 currently-quiet products.
- **Two of my own hypotheses were disproved by measurement and discarded rather than reported**: unnormalized embeddings / L2-vs-cosine mismatch (norms are exactly 1.0; L2 and cosine top-20 overlap 100%) and mechanical header/signature noise contaminating embeddings (0% noise in the visible 256-token window).

**Fase 1 — GPU-only fail-fast + gate telemetry (ADR-0059, ADR-0060).**
- `main.py` wires **only** `LlamaCppClient` for gate and scorer. All Groq/Ollama/`FallbackLLMClient` imports and wiring removed, with a comment block recording why (silent model substitution with no audit trail; the gate has no per-call provider field; a fallback run takes many hours). **The modules and their tests stay in the tree, unwired at exactly one place.**
- `LlamaCppClient` gained `health_check()`, `provider_label`, and **`LlamaCppTruncated` raised on `finish_reason == "length"`** — truncation was previously invisible (HTTP 200 with a partial body). It is deliberately **not** in `_PRIMARY_FAILURES`: a prompt-sizing problem must not trigger a provider switch.
- `AssessmentService._preflight_llm()` probes before extraction and aborts the whole run if the server is down; documents already extracted/indexed are kept; a mid-run infrastructure failure aborts while preserving Ações already evaluated; an error in one Ação's own evaluation is recorded and the run continues. **`_gate_chunk` deliberately does NOT catch transport failures** — swallowing them would produce a complete-looking result built on a handful of surviving chunks.
- New telemetry: `RelevanceVerdict.failure_reason`, `RejectedChunk.gate_failed`/`gate_failure_reason`/`gate_provider`/`gate_model`, and `ProductGateReport` per Expected Product (`n_candidates`/`n_examined`/`n_accepted`/`n_rejected`/`n_gate_failures`/`providers`/`models`).

**Fase 2 — the score is now counted in code (ADR-0058).**
- `build_user_prompt` emits a block for **every** scored product, empty ones marked `NENHUMA evidência foi recuperada para este Produto Esperado.`, with a header instruction stating an empty block means NOT evidenced; the summary line names the unevidenced products. `_build_scoring_instruction` now states the product count explicitly ("conte quantos dos {n}").
- `SCORER_SCHEMA` gained a **required** `products` array (`product_id`/`evidenced`/`justificativa`), **listed first** so grammar-constrained generation makes the model commit per product before naming a score.
- `parse_llm_response(raw, expected_product_ids=...)` **computes** the score from the verdicts when they cover exactly the expected id set (`score_source="computed"`). Anything less — absent, malformed, duplicated, subset, unknown ids — falls back to the model's own `score` and **forces `uncertainty_flag=True`**. It never becomes `parse_failed`: a parse failure would discard a whole Ação's work. One-line revert documented in `schemas.py`.
- `EvaluationResult` gained `product_verdicts` and `score_source`, both defaulted. **No migration needed**: the read path is `json.loads(raw_json)` (ADR-0031) and does not re-validate, verified by a test that strips the new keys from a stored row and asserts the API still serves it.
- `AcaoPanel.vue` gained a per-product coverage table (verdict + gate counters joined by product id), a **warning alert when `n_gate_failures > 0`** stating the score may be understated, and a note of which path produced the score. All new fields read through `?? []`, and the `score_source === 'model'` alert is gated on `product_gate_reports` being present so older rows stay quiet. `npm run build` clean.

**Tests: 432 → 489 fast, all passing.** New: `tests/test_gate_telemetry.py` (13), `tests/test_assessment_resilience.py` (9), +13 in `test_llamacpp_client.py`, +11 in `test_evaluate_orchestration.py`, +9 in `test_prompt_builder.py`, +3 in `test_lifecycle_integrity.py`. One pre-existing test (`test_normal_evaluation_score_3`) legitimately broke on the new contract and was updated to it, with both the computed and the fallback path now covered explicitly.

**Fase 3 — BLOCKED ON THE GPU MACHINE, nothing applied.** Each item is deliberately gated on measurement rather than applied blind: **3.1** raise the gate's `max_tokens` (or add a `reasoning` field to `GATE_SCHEMA`) **only if** the new `finish_reason` counter proves truncation is happening; **3.2** check the server's real `n_ctx` via `GET /props` before touching `_MAX_EVIDENCE_CHARS` (21k was derived from Groq's rate limit, not from this server); **3.3** `_cap_evidence` penalises `cascade_step="vector"`, which is wrong now that vector is co-equal (ADR-0049); **3.4** `_MAX_EXAMINED` 8 → 16; **3.5** revisit the 35 oversized `negative_evidence_patterns` **one at a time, never in bulk**.
- **First action once the GPU is back**: `scripts/diagnostics/reproducibility.py --runs 10` — the acceptance test for the user's ≥90% modal-agreement criterion. Do this *before* any Fase 3 tuning, so there is a post-Fase-1/2 baseline to compare against.
